### src/components/prayers/AddCategoryDialog.vue
```
<template>
  <v-dialog
    :model-value="modelValue"
    @update:model-value="$emit('update:modelValue', $event)"
    max-width="600px"
    scrollable
  >
    <v-card>
      <v-card-title>Дадаць катэгорыю</v-card-title>
      <v-card-text>
        <v-form @submit.prevent="onAddCategory">
          <v-text-field
            v-model="newCategory.name"
            label="Назва катэгорыі"
            variant="outlined"
            class="mb-4"
            required
          ></v-text-field>
          <v-combobox
            v-model="newCategory.tags"
            :items="allTags"
            label="Тэгі для фільтрацыі"
            variant="outlined"
            multiple
            chips
            clearable
            required
          ></v-combobox>
        </v-form>
      </v-card-text>
      <v-card-actions>
        <v-spacer></v-spacer>
        <v-btn variant="text" @click="$emit('update:modelValue', false)">
          Скасаваць
        </v-btn>
        <v-btn 
          color="primary" 
          variant="flat" 
          @click="onAddCategory"
          :disabled="!isValid"
        >
          Дадаць
        </v-btn>
      </v-card-actions>
    </v-card>
  </v-dialog>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settings';
import { usePrayers } from '@/composables/usePrayers';
import { useNotifier } from '@/composables/useNotifier';
import { validateCategory } from '@/utils/validators';

defineProps({ modelValue: Boolean });
const emit = defineEmits(['update:modelValue', 'category-added']);

const settings = useSettingsStore();
const { allTags } = usePrayers();
const { notifySuccess, notifyError } = useNotifier();
const newCategory = ref({ name: '', tags: [] });

const isValid = computed(() => {
  const validation = validateCategory(newCategory.value);
  return validation === true;
});

function onAddCategory() {
  const validation = validateCategory(newCategory.value);
  if (validation !== true) {
    notifyError(validation);
    return;
  }
  
  try {
    settings.addCategory({ ...newCategory.value });
    notifySuccess('Катэгорыя дададзена');
    newCategory.value = { name: '', tags: [] };
    // Закрываем диалог
    emit('update:modelValue', false);
    // Также эмитим событие для совместимости
    emit('category-added');
  } catch (error) {
    notifyError(error.message || 'Памылка пры дадаванні катэгорыі');
  }
}
</script>


```

### src/components/prayers/PrayerBar.vue
```
<template>
  <v-app-bar flat>
    <v-app-bar-nav-icon @click="goBack">
      <v-icon>mdi-arrow-left</v-icon>
    </v-app-bar-nav-icon>

    <v-app-bar-title></v-app-bar-title>

    <v-spacer></v-spacer>
 
    <v-btn icon @click="toggleSettingsSheet">
      <v-icon>mdi-format-font</v-icon>
    </v-btn>

    <v-btn 
      v-if="auth && auth.isAdmin" 
      icon 
      @click="toggleEdit"
      :loading="saving"
    >
      <v-icon>{{ isEditing ? 'mdi-check' : 'mdi-pencil-outline' }}</v-icon>
    </v-btn>
    
  </v-app-bar>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, onUnmounted } from 'vue';
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useAppBar } from '@/composables/useAppBar';
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth.js';

const router = useRouter();
const { toggleSettingsSheet } = useAppBar();
const auth = useAuthStore();

// Используем глобальное событие для связи с PrayerView
const isEditing = ref(false);
const saving = ref(false);

function handleStateUpdate(e) {
  isEditing.value = e.detail.isEditing;
  saving.value = e.detail.saving;
}

onMounted(() => {
  window.addEventListener('prayer-state-update', handleStateUpdate);
});

onUnmounted(() => {
  window.removeEventListener('prayer-state-update', handleStateUpdate);
});

const goBack = () => {
  if (window.history.length > 1) {
    router.go(-1);
  } else {
    router.push({ name: 'PrayersList' });
  }
};

const toggleEdit = () => {
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('prayer-toggle-edit'));
};
</script>

```

### src/components/prayers/PrayerForm.vue
```
<template>
  <v-container>
    <v-form @submit.prevent="handleSave">
      <v-text-field
        v-model="form.title"
        label="Назва"
        variant="outlined"
        class="mb-4"
        :rules="[validatePrayerTitle]"
        counter="200"
        required
      ></v-text-field>

      <v-textarea
        v-model="form.text"
        label="Тэкст малітвы"
        variant="outlined"
        rows="10"
        class="mb-4"
        :rules="[validatePrayerText]"
        required
      ></v-textarea>

      <v-combobox
        v-model="form.tags"
        :items="allTags"
        label="Тэгі"
        multiple
        chips
        clearable
        variant="outlined"
        class="mb-4"
      ></v-combobox>

      <v-btn
        type="submit"
        color="primary"
        variant="flat"
        :loading="saving"
        block
      >
        {{ isEditMode ? 'Захаваць змены' : 'Дадаць малітву' }}
      </v-btn>
    </v-form>
  </v-container>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, watch, onMounted } from 'vue';
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { usePrayers } from '@/composables/usePrayers';
import { useNotifier } from '@/composables/useNotifier';
import { validatePrayerTitle, validatePrayerText } from '@/utils/validators';

const route = useRoute();
const router = useRouter();
const { prayers, addPrayer, updatePrayer, allTags } = usePrayers();
const { notifySuccess, notifyError } = useNotifier();

const isEditMode = computed(() => !!route.params.id);
const saving = ref(false);

const form = ref({
  title: '',
  text: '',
  tags: []
});

onMounted(() => {
  if (isEditMode.value) {
    const prayer = prayers.value.find(p => p.id === route.params.id);
    if (!prayer) {
      notifyError('Малітва не знойдзена');
      router.push({ name: 'PrayersList' });
      return;
    }
    
    // Поддержка старого формата с titleVersions/textVersions и нового формата
    if (prayer.titleVersions) {
      form.value.title = prayer.titleVersions.be || prayer.titleVersions.ru || Object.values(prayer.titleVersions)[0] || '';
    } else {
      form.value.title = prayer.title || '';
    }
    
    if (prayer.textVersions) {
      form.value.text = prayer.textVersions.be || prayer.textVersions.ru || Object.values(prayer.textVersions)[0] || '';
    } else {
      form.value.text = prayer.text || '';
    }
    
    form.value.tags = prayer.tags || [];
  }
});

async function handleSave() {
  // Валидация
  const titleValidation = validatePrayerTitle(form.value.title);
  const textValidation = validatePrayerText(form.value.text);
  
  if (titleValidation !== true) {
    notifyError(titleValidation);
    return;
  }
  
  if (textValidation !== true) {
    notifyError(textValidation);
    return;
  }

  saving.value = true;
  try {
    if (isEditMode.value) {
      await updatePrayer(route.params.id, form.value);
      notifySuccess('Малітва абноўлена');
    } else {
      await addPrayer(form.value);
      notifySuccess('Малітва дададзена');
    }
    router.push({ name: 'PrayersList' });
  } catch (error) {
    const { handleFirestoreError } = await import('@/utils/errorHandler');
    handleFirestoreError(error, 'PrayerForm');
  } finally {
    saving.value = false;
  }
}
</script>


```

### src/components/prayers/PrayersList.vue

<template>
  <v-container class="prayers-list-container">
    <div class="d-flex align-center justify-space-between mb-4">
      <h1 class="text-h5 font-weight-light mb-0">{{ selectedCategory?.name || 'Малітвы' }}</h1>
      <v-chip 
        v-if="!isLoading && filteredPrayers.length > 0" 
        size="small" 
        color="primary" 
        variant="tonal"
      >
        {{ filteredPrayers.length }} {{ filteredPrayers.length === 1 ? 'малітва' : 'малітваў' }}
      </v-chip>
    </div>

    <!-- Кнопки поиска и фильтра -->
    <div class="d-flex align-center ga-2 mb-4">

        :color="isSearchExpanded ? 'primary' : 'default'"
        variant="flat"
        size="large"
        @click="isSearchExpanded = !isSearchExpanded"
        aria-label="Пошук"
      >
        <v-icon>mdi-magnify</v-icon>
      </v-btn>
      
      <v-btn
        icon
        :color="isFilterExpanded ? 'primary' : 'default'"
        variant="flat"
        size="large"
        @click="isFilterExpanded = !isFilterExpanded"
        aria-label="Фільтр"
      >
        <v-icon>mdi-filter</v-icon>
      </v-btn>
    </div>

    <!-- Раскрывающаяся панель поиска -->
    <v-expand-transition>
      <v-text-field
        v-if="isSearchExpanded"
        v-model="searchQuery"
        label="Пошук малітваў"
        prepend-inner-icon="mdi-magnify"
        variant="outlined"
        density="compact"
        hide-details
        class="mb-4 search-field"
        clearable
        @click:clear="searchQuery = ''"
      ></v-text-field>
    </v-expand-transition>

    <!-- Раскрывающаяся панель фильтра -->
    <v-expand-transition>
      <v-combobox
        v-if="isFilterExpanded"
        v-model="selectedTags"
        :items="allTags"
        label="Фільтр па тэгах"
        multiple
        chips
        clearable
        variant="outlined"
        density="compact"
        hide-details
        class="mb-4 filter-field"
      ></v-combobox>
    </v-expand-transition>

    <div v-if="isLoading">
      <v-skeleton-loader v-for="n in 3" :key="n" type="card" class="mb-4"></v-skeleton-loader>
    </div>
    
    <v-alert v-else-if="error" type="error" class="mb-4">
      <div class="text-h6">Памылка загрузкі малітваў</div>
      <p>{{ error.message || error }}</p>
      <p v-if="error.code === 'failed-precondition'" class="text-caption mt-2">
        Магчыма, патрабуецца стварыць індэкс у Firebase Console для запыту.
      </p>
    </v-alert>
    
    <div v-else>
      <v-list lines="one" bg-color="transparent">
        <v-list-item
          v-for="prayer in filteredPrayers"
          :key="prayer.id"
          @click="viewPrayer(prayer.id)"

        >
          <template v-slot:prepend>
            <v-btn
              icon
              variant="text"
              size="small"
              @click.stop="toggleFavorite(prayer.id)"
              :color="isFavorite(prayer.id) ? 'red' : 'grey'"
              :aria-label="isFavorite(prayer.id) ? 'Выдаліць з абраных' : 'Дадаць у абраныя'"
            >
              <v-icon>{{ isFavorite(prayer.id) ? 'mdi-heart' : 'mdi-heart-outline' }}</v-icon>
            </v-btn>
          </template>

          <v-list-item-title class="prayer-title-text">
            {{ getTitle(prayer) }}
          </v-list-item-title>

          <template v-slot:append>
            <div class="d-flex align-center ga-1">
              <v-btn
                v-if="authStore.isAdmin"
                icon="mdi-pencil"
                variant="text"
                size="small"
                @click.stop="router.push({ name: 'PrayerEdit', params: { id: prayer.id } })"
                aria-label="Рэдагаваць малітву"
              ></v-btn>
              <v-btn
                v-if="authStore.isAdmin"
                icon="mdi-delete"
                variant="text"
                size="small"
                color="error"
                @click.stop="openDeleteDialog(prayer)"
                aria-label="Выдаліць малітву"
              ></v-btn>
            </div>
          </template>
        </v-list-item>
      </v-list>

      <div v-if="!isLoading && filteredPrayers.length === 0" class="text-center text-grey-darken-1 mt-16">
        <v-icon size="48" class="mb-2">mdi-note-off-outline</v-icon>
        <p>Малітвы не знойдзены</p>
        <p v-if="searchQuery || selectedTags.length > 0" class="text-caption mt-2">
          Паспрабуйце змяніць пошук або фільтры
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>

    <v-btn
      v-if="authStore.isAdmin"
      icon="mdi-plus"
      color="primary"
      position="fixed"
      location="bottom right"
      size="large"
      class="add-prayer-btn"
      @click="router.push({ name: 'PrayerAdd' })"
    ></v-btn>

    <!-- Диалог подтверждения удаления -->
    <v-dialog v-model="deleteDialogOpen" max-width="400">
      <v-card>
        <v-card-title class="text-h6">Выдаліць малітву?</v-card-title>
        <v-card-text>
          <p>Вы ўпэўнены, што хочаце выдаліць малітву "{{ prayerToDelete ? getTitle(prayerToDelete) : '' }}"?</p>
          <p class="text-caption text-error mt-2">Гэта дзеянне нельга адмяніць.</p>
        </v-card-text>
        <v-card-actions>
          <v-spacer></v-spacer>
          <v-btn variant="text" @click="closeDeleteDialog">Скасаваць</v-btn>
          <v-btn color="error" variant="flat" @click="confirmDelete">Выдаліць</v-btn>
        </v-card-actions>
      </v-card>
    </v-dialog>
  </v-container>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, watch } from 'vue';
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { usePrayers } from '@/composables/usePrayers';
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settings';
import { useDebounce } from '@/composables/useDebounce';
import { useNotifier } from '@/composables/useNotifier';

const props = defineProps({
  selectedCategory: {
    type: Object,
    default: null
  }
});

const router = useRouter();
const { prayers, isLoading, getTitle, getText, allTags, error, deletePrayer } = usePrayers();
const authStore = useAuthStore();
const settings = useSettingsStore();
const { notifySuccess, notifyError } = useNotifier();

const searchQuery = ref('');
const debouncedSearchQuery = useDebounce(searchQuery, 300);
const selectedTags = ref([]);
const isSearchExpanded = ref(false);
const isFilterExpanded = ref(false);
const deleteDialogOpen = ref(false);
const prayerToDelete = ref(null);

// Применяем фильтр категории при изменении
watch(() => props.selectedCategory, (category) => {
  if (category && category.tags) {
    selectedTags.value = [...category.tags];
  } else {
    selectedTags.value = [];
  }
}, { immediate: true });

const filteredPrayers = computed(() => {
  if (isLoading.value) return [];
  
  const prayersArray = prayers.value;
  if (!prayersArray || !Array.isArray(prayersArray)) {
    return [];
  }
  
  let filtered = [...prayersArray];
  
  // Фильтр по тегам категории
  if (selectedTags.value.length > 0) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(prayer => {
      const prayerTags = prayer.tags || [];
      return selectedTags.value.every(tag => prayerTags.includes(tag));
    });
  }
  
  // Поиск
  if (debouncedSearchQuery.value.trim()) {
    const query = debouncedSearchQuery.value.toLowerCase().trim();
    filtered = filtered.filter(prayer => {
      const title = getTitle(prayer).toLowerCase();
      const text = getText(prayer);
      const textPlain = text.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '').toLowerCase();
      return title.includes(query) || textPlain.includes(query);
    });
  }
  
  // Сортировка: избранные вверху, затем по алфавиту
  filtered.sort((a, b) => {
    const aIsFavorite = settings.favoritePrayers.includes(a.id);
    const bIsFavorite = settings.favoritePrayers.includes(b.id);
    
    // Избранные всегда вверху
    if (aIsFavorite && !bIsFavorite) return -1;
    if (!aIsFavorite && bIsFavorite) return 1;
    
    // Затем сортировка по алфавиту
    const titleA = getTitle(a).toLowerCase();
    const titleB = getTitle(b).toLowerCase();
    return titleA.localeCompare(titleB, 'be', { sensitivity: 'base' });
  });
  
  return filtered;
});

function viewPrayer(id) {
  router.push({ name: 'PrayerView', params: { id } });
}

function isFavorite(prayerId) {
  return settings.favoritePrayers.includes(prayerId);
}

function toggleFavorite(prayerId) {
  settings.toggleFavoritePrayer(prayerId);
}

function openDeleteDialog(prayer) {
  prayerToDelete.value = prayer;
  deleteDialogOpen.value = true;
}

function closeDeleteDialog() {
  deleteDialogOpen.value = false;
  prayerToDelete.value = null;
}

async function confirmDelete() {
  if (!prayerToDelete.value) return;
  
  try {
    await deletePrayer(prayerToDelete.value.id);
    notifySuccess('Малітва выдалена');
    closeDeleteDialog();
  } catch (error) {
    notifyError('Памылка пры выдаленні малітвы');
  }
}
</script>

<style scoped>

### src/components/prayers/PrayerView.vue

<template>
  <v-container v-if="prayer" class="prayer-view-container">
    <div class="prayer-content-area">
      <!-- Режим редактирования -->

          :rules="[validatePrayerText]"
          required
        ></v-textarea>

          v-model="editForm.tags"
          :items="allTags"
          label="Тэгі"
          multiple
          chips
          clearable
          variant="outlined"
          class="mb-4"
        ></v-combobox>
      </template>

      <!-- Режим просмотра -->
      <template v-else>
        <h1 class="text-h4 font-weight-light mb-4 prayer-title" v-html="getTitle(prayer)"></h1>
        
        <div v-if="prayer.tags && prayer.tags.length > 0" class="mb-4">
          <v-chip
            v-for="tag in prayer.tags"
            :key="tag"
            size="small"
            color="primary"
            variant="tonal"
            class="mr-2 mb-2"
          >
            {{ tag }}
          </v-chip>
        </div>

      </template>

  </v-container>

    <v-progress-circular indeterminate color="primary"></v-progress-circular>
    <p class="mt-4">Загрузка малітвы...</p>
  </v-container>
  
  <v-container v-else class="text-center">
    <v-icon size="64" class="mb-4" color="grey">mdi-file-question-outline</v-icon>
    <h2 class="text-h5 mb-4">Малітва не знойдзена</h2>
    <p class="text-medium-emphasis mb-6">Малітва з такім ID не існуе або была выдалена</p>
    <v-btn color="primary" :to="{ name: 'PrayersList' }">Вярнуцца да спісу малітваў</v-btn>
  </v-container>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, watch, onUnmounted, onMounted, provide } from 'vue';
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { usePrayers } from '@/composables/usePrayers';
import { useNotifier } from '@/composables/useNotifier';
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth';
import { validatePrayerTitle, validatePrayerText } from '@/utils/validators';
import { logger } from '@/utils/logger';

const route = useRoute();
const router = useRouter();
const { prayers, isLoading, getTitle, getText, updatePrayer, allTags } = usePrayers();
const { notifySuccess, notifyError } = useNotifier();
const authStore = useAuthStore();

const prayer = computed(() => {
  return prayers.value.find(p => p.id === route.params.id);
});

const isEditing = ref(false);
const saving = ref(false);

const editForm = ref({
  title: '',
  text: '',
  tags: []
});

// Предоставляем функции для PrayerBar
provide('prayerView', {
  isEditing,
  toggleEdit: () => toggleEdit(),
  saving
});

watch(prayer, (newPrayer) => {
  if (newPrayer && isEditing.value) {
    loadPrayerData();
  }
}, { immediate: true });

// Слушаем события от PrayerBar и отправляем обновления состояния
onMounted(() => {
  window.addEventListener('prayer-toggle-edit', toggleEdit);
  
  // Отправляем начальное состояние
  updatePrayerBarState();
});

onUnmounted(() => {
  window.removeEventListener('prayer-toggle-edit', toggleEdit);
});

// Отслеживаем изменения состояния и отправляем в PrayerBar
watch([isEditing, saving], () => {
  updatePrayerBarState();
});

function updatePrayerBarState() {
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('prayer-state-update', {
    detail: {
      isEditing: isEditing.value,
      saving: saving.value
    }
  }));
}

function loadPrayerData() {
  if (!prayer.value) return;
  
  if (prayer.value.titleVersions) {
    editForm.value.title = prayer.value.titleVersions.be || prayer.value.titleVersions.ru || Object.values(prayer.value.titleVersions)[0] || '';
  } else {
    editForm.value.title = prayer.value.title || '';
  }
  
  if (prayer.value.textVersions) {
    editForm.value.text = prayer.value.textVersions.be || prayer.value.textVersions.ru || Object.values(prayer.value.textVersions)[0] || '';
  } else {
    editForm.value.text = prayer.value.text || '';
  }
  
  editForm.value.tags = prayer.value.tags || [];
}

function toggleEdit() {
  if (isEditing.value) {
    // Сохраняем изменения
    savePrayer();
  } else {
    // Включаем режим редактирования
    loadPrayerData();
    isEditing.value = true;
  }
}

async function savePrayer() {
  const titleValidation = validatePrayerTitle(editForm.value.title);
  const textValidation = validatePrayerText(editForm.value.text);
  
  if (titleValidation !== true) {
    notifyError(titleValidation);
    return;
  }
  
  if (textValidation !== true) {
    notifyError(textValidation);
    return;
  }

  saving.value = true;
  try {
    await updatePrayer(route.params.id, editForm.value);
    notifySuccess('Малітва абноўлена');
    isEditing.value = false;
  } catch (error) {
    notifyError('Памылка пры абнаўленні малітвы');
    logger.error('Error updating prayer:', error);
  } finally {
    saving.value = false;
  }
}

// Экспортируем для использования в PrayerBar
defineExpose({
  isEditing,
  toggleEdit
});

let copyTimeoutId = null;

async function copyText() {
  if (!prayer.value) return;
  
  if (copyTimeoutId) {
    clearTimeout(copyTimeoutId);
  }
  
  const title = getTitle(prayer.value);
  const text = getText(prayer.value);
  
  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(text, 'text/html');
  const plainText = doc.body.textContent || text;
  
  const textToCopy = `${title}\n\n${plainText}`;
  
  try {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(textToCopy);
    copySuccess.value = true;
    notifySuccess('Тэкст скапіяваны');
    copyTimeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
      copySuccess.value = false;
      copyTimeoutId = null;
    }, 2000);
  } catch (error) {
    const textArea = document.createElement('textarea');
    textArea.value = textToCopy;
    textArea.style.position = 'fixed';
    textArea.style.opacity = '0';
    document.body.appendChild(textArea);
    textArea.select();
    try {
      document.execCommand('copy');
      copySuccess.value = true;
      notifySuccess('Тэкст скапіяваны');
      copyTimeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
        copySuccess.value = false;
        copyTimeoutId = null;
      }, 2000);
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error('Не атрымалася скапіяваць тэкст:', err);
    } finally {
      document.body.removeChild(textArea);
    }
  }
}

onUnmounted(() => {
  if (copyTimeoutId) {
    clearTimeout(copyTimeoutId);
  }
});

watch([isLoading, prayer], ([loading, foundPrayer]) => {
  if (!loading && !foundPrayer && prayers.value.length > 0) {
  }
});
</script>

<template>
  <v-container class="readings-container">
    <div v-if="isLoading" class="text-center py-8">
      <v-progress-circular indeterminate color="primary" size="64"></v-progress-circular>
      <p class="mt-4 text-grey">Загрузка чытанняў...</p>
    </div>

    <v-alert v-else-if="error" type="error" class="mb-4">
      <div class="text-h6">Памылка загрузкі чытанняў</div>
      <p>{{ error.message || error }}</p>
    </v-alert>

    <div v-else-if="hasReading" class="readings-content">
      <div class="prayer-content-area" v-html="readingText"></div>
    </div>

    <div v-else class="text-center py-16">
      <v-icon size="64" color="grey-lighten-1">mdi-book-open-variant-outline</v-icon>
      <p class="text-h6 mt-4 text-grey">Чытанні для гэтага дня не знойдзены</p>
      <p class="text-caption mt-2 text-grey-darken-1">
        Магчыма, яны яшчэ не дададзены ў базу даных
      </p>
    </div>
  </v-container>
</template>

<script setup>
import { onMounted, watch } from 'vue';
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router';
import { useReadings } from '@/composables/useReadings';
import { useLiturgicalDay } from '@/composables/useLiturgicalDay';
import { getLiturgicalYear, getCycleForDate } from '@/utils/liturgicalCycle';
import { logger } from '@/utils/logger';
import moment from 'moment';

const route = useRoute();
const { 
  readingText, 
  hasReading, 
  isLoading, 
  error, 
  loadReadingsForDate 
} = useReadings();

const { getLiturgicalOptionsForDate } = useLiturgicalDay();

/**
 * Загрузить чтения для текущей даты
 */
async function loadReadings() {
  // Поддержка параметра date из роута или query
  const dateParam = route.params.date || route.query.date;
  const date = dateParam ? moment(dateParam, 'YYYY-MM-DD').toDate() : moment().toDate();
  
  logger.log('[ReadingsView] Загрузка чытанняў для даты:', moment(date).format('YYYY-MM-DD'));
  
  try {
    // Получаем литургический контекст для даты
    const liturgicalData = await getLiturgicalOptionsForDate(date);
    const context = liturgicalData?.context || {};
    
    // Определяем цикл (используем calendarStore для точности)
    const liturgicalYear = await getLiturgicalYear(date, { useCalendarStore: true });
    const cycle = await getCycleForDate(date, liturgicalYear, context.dayOfWeek, { useCalendarStore: true });
    
    // Загружаем чтения с контекстом
    await loadReadingsForDate(date, {
      cycle,
      periodType: context.periodType,
      periodWeekNumber: context.periodWeek,
      dayOfWeek: context.dayOfWeek,
      feastId: liturgicalData?.items?.[0]?._feastId,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error('[ReadingsView] Памылка загрузкі чытанняў:', err);
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  loadReadings();
});

watch(() => route.params.date || route.query.date, () => {
  loadReadings();
});
</script>

}

  width: 100%;
}

### src/composables/useAppBar.js
```
import { ref } from 'vue';

const isSettingsSheetOpen = ref(false);
const isDrawerOpen = ref(false);
const isCalendarDialogOpen = ref(false);

export function useAppBar() {
  const toggleSettingsSheet = () => {
    isSettingsSheetOpen.value = !isSettingsSheetOpen.value;
  };
  const toggleCalendarDialog = () => {
    isCalendarDialogOpen.value = !isCalendarDialogOpen.value;
  };
  return {
    isDrawerOpen,
    isSettingsSheetOpen,
    isCalendarDialogOpen,
    toggleSettingsSheet,
    toggleCalendarDialog
  };
}
```

### src/composables/useDebounce.js
```
import { ref, watch, onUnmounted } from 'vue';

export function useDebounce(source, delay = 300) {
  const debounced = ref(source.value);
  let timeoutId = null;

  const stopWatcher = watch(source, (newValue) => {
    if (timeoutId) {
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    }
    timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
      debounced.value = newValue;
    }, delay);
  }, { immediate: true });

  onUnmounted(() => {
    stopWatcher();
    if (timeoutId) {
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    }
  });

  return debounced;
}


```

### src/composables/useLiturgicalDay.js
```
import { collection, query, where, getDocs } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { useFirestore } from 'vuefire';
import { useCalendarStore } from '@/stores/calendar';
import moment from 'moment';
import 'moment/dist/locale/be';
import { db } from '@/plugins/firebase';
import { retryFirestore } from '@/utils/retry';
import { logger } from '@/utils/logger';
import { getLiturgicalOptionsFromCache, saveLiturgicalOptionsToCache } from '@/utils/liturgicalCache'; 

export function useLiturgicalDay() {
    const calendarStore = useCalendarStore();
    const daysCollection = collection(db, 'days');
    moment.locale('be');
    
    const feastsCache = {};

    const feastNameMap = {
        holyFamily: 'holyfamily',
        baptismOfLord: 'buptismofthelord',
        holyTrinity: 'trinity',
        christKing: 'christking',
        sacredHeartOfJesus: 'heartofjesus',
        immaculateHeartOfMary: 'heartofmary',
        christTheHighPriest: 'christthehighpriest',
        ashWednesday: 'ash',
        easterSunday: 'eastersunday',
        pentecost: 'pentecost',
        palmsSnday: 'palmsunday',
        corpusChristi: 'palmsunday',
        ascension: 'ascension'
    };

    const getMoment = (isoDateStr) => moment(isoDateStr, 'YYYY-MM-DD');

    const getItemsForPeriodQuery = (type, day, week) => {
        return query(daysCollection,
            where("_periodType", "==", type),
            where("_dayOfWeek", "==", `${day}`),
            where("_periodWeekNumber", "==", `${week}`)
        );
    };

    const getPeriodQuery = (mDate, year) => {
        const opts = calendarStore.optionsForYear(year).value;
        const nextYearOpts = calendarStore.optionsForYear(year + 1).value;
        if (!opts) return null;

        const fa = getMoment(opts.firstAdventSunday);
        const christmas = moment(`${year}-12-25`, "YYYY-MM-DD");

        // --- Advent ---
        if (mDate.isSameOrAfter(fa) && mDate.isBefore(christmas)) {
            const diff = mDate.diff(fa, "days");
            return getItemsForPeriodQuery("advent", diff % 7 + 1, Math.floor(diff / 7) + 1);
        }

        // --- Christmas Time ---
        if (nextYearOpts && mDate.isSameOrAfter(christmas)) {
            const flNext = getMoment(nextYearOpts.ordintaryStart1);
            const weeksBefore = parseInt(nextYearOpts.ordintaryWeeks1);
            const lastChristmas = flNext.clone().subtract(weeksBefore, 'weeks').subtract(2, 'days');
            if (mDate.isSameOrBefore(lastChristmas)) {
                const diff = mDate.diff(christmas, "days");
                return getItemsForPeriodQuery("christmas", diff % 7 + 1, Math.floor(diff / 7) + 1);
            }
        }

        const easter = getMoment(opts.easterSunday);
        const ash = getMoment(opts.ashWednesday);

        // --- Lent & Holy Week ---
        if (mDate.isSameOrAfter(ash) && mDate.isBefore(easter)) {
            const firstLentSunday = ash.clone().add(4, "days");
            const lastLentSunday = easter.clone().subtract(1, "week");
            const startDiff = mDate.diff(firstLentSunday, "days");
            const endDiff = mDate.diff(lastLentSunday, "days");

            if (startDiff < 0) return getItemsForPeriodQuery('lent', 7 + startDiff + 1, 0);
            if (endDiff > 0 && endDiff < 8) return getItemsForPeriodQuery('holyweek', endDiff + 1, 1);
            if (endDiff === 0) return query(daysCollection, where('_feast', '==', 'palmSunday'));
            return getItemsForPeriodQuery('lent', startDiff % 7 + 1, Math.floor(startDiff / 7) + 1);
        }

        const pentecost = getMoment(opts.pentecost);

        // --- Easter Time ---
        if (mDate.isSameOrAfter(easter) && mDate.isSameOrBefore(pentecost)) {
            const diff = mDate.diff(easter, "days");
            return getItemsForPeriodQuery("easter", diff % 7 + 1, Math.floor(diff / 7) + 1);
        }

        // --- Ordinary Time ---
        const weeksBeforeOrd = parseInt(opts.ordintaryWeeks1) - 1;
        const fo = ash.clone().subtract(2, "days").subtract(weeksBeforeOrd, "weeks");
        if (mDate.isSameOrAfter(fo) && mDate.isBefore(ash)) {
            const diff = mDate.diff(fo, "days");
            return getItemsForPeriodQuery("ordinary", diff % 7 + 1, Math.floor(diff / 7) + 1);
        }

        if (mDate.isAfter(pentecost) && mDate.isBefore(fa)) {
            const diff = mDate.diff(pentecost, "days");
            const startWeek = parseInt(opts.ordintaryWeeks2);
            return getItemsForPeriodQuery("ordinary", diff % 7 + 1, Math.floor(diff / 7) + startWeek);
        }

        return null;
    };

    async function computeLiturgicalOptionsForDate(currentDate) {
        const mDate = moment(currentDate).startOf('day');
        const year = mDate.year();

        await calendarStore.getYearOptions(year);
        await calendarStore.getYearOptions(year + 1);
        await calendarStore.getDynamicDates(year);

        const periodQuery = getPeriodQuery(mDate, year);
        const fixedFeastQuery = getFixedFeastQuery(mDate);
        const dynamicFeastsQueries = getDynamicFeastsQueries(mDate, year);

        const context = {
            periodType: 'ordinary',
            periodWeek: -1,
            dayOfWeek: mDate.isoWeekday()
        };

        if (periodQuery && periodQuery._query) {
            const typeFilter = periodQuery._query.filters.find(f => f.field.segments.includes('_periodType'));
            const weekFilter = periodQuery._query.filters.find(f => f.field.segments.includes('_periodWeekNumber'));
            
            context.periodType = typeFilter?.value.stringValue || 'ordinary';
            context.periodWeek = parseInt(weekFilter?.value.stringValue || '-1');
        }

        const queryPromises = [periodQuery, fixedFeastQuery, ...dynamicFeastsQueries].filter(p => p);
        const querySnapshots = await Promise.all(
            queryPromises.map(q => retryFirestore(() => getDocs(q)))
        );

        const results = new Map();
        querySnapshots.forEach(snapshot => {
            snapshot.forEach(doc => {
                if (!results.has(doc.id)) {
                    results.set(doc.id, { id: doc.id, ...doc.data() });
                }
            });
        });
        
        return {
            items: Array.from(results.values()),
            context: context
        };
    }

    async function getLiturgicalOptionsForDate(currentDate) {
        const mDate = moment(currentDate).startOf('day');
        const dateObj = mDate.toDate();
        
        const cachedOptions = await getLiturgicalOptionsFromCache(dateObj);
        if (cachedOptions) {
            return cachedOptions;
        }
        
        logger.log(`[LiturgicalDay] Вылічэнне опцый для ${mDate.format('YYYY-MM-DD')}`);
        const options = await computeLiturgicalOptionsForDate(currentDate);
        
        saveLiturgicalOptionsToCache(dateObj, options).catch(error => {
            logger.error(`[LiturgicalDay] Не ўдалося захаваць у кэш:`, error);
        });
        
        return options;
    }

    const getFixedFeastQuery = (mDate) => {
        return query(daysCollection, where('_date', '==', mDate.format('D.M')));
    };

     function getDynamicFeastsQueries(mDate, year) {
        const dynamicDates = calendarStore.dynamicDatesForYear(year).value || {};
        const queries = [];
        for (const feastKey in dynamicDates) {
            if (feastNameMap[feastKey] && dynamicDates[feastKey].isSame(mDate, 'day')) {
                queries.push(query(daysCollection, where('_feast', '==', feastNameMap[feastKey])));
            }
        }
        return queries;
    }

    async function runLiturgicalDiagnostics(currentDate) {
        const mDate = moment(currentDate).startOf('day');
        const year = mDate.year();

        await calendarStore.getYearOptions(year);
        await calendarStore.getYearOptions(year + 1);
        await calendarStore.getDynamicDates(year);

        const diagnostics = {};
        const periodQuery = getPeriodQuery(mDate, year);
        if (periodQuery) {
            diagnostics.periodParams = {
                periodType: periodQuery._query.filters.find(f => f.field.segments.includes('_periodType'))?.value.stringValue,
                periodWeek: parseInt(periodQuery._query.filters.find(f => f.field.segments.includes('_periodWeekNumber'))?.value.stringValue),
                dayOfWeek: parseInt(periodQuery._query.filters.find(f => f.field.segments.includes('_dayOfWeek'))?.value.stringValue)
            };
            const periodSnapshot = await getDocs(periodQuery);
            diagnostics.periodResult = periodSnapshot.docs.map(doc => ({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
        } else {
            diagnostics.periodResult = [];
            diagnostics.periodParams = null;
        }

        const fixedFeastQuery = getFixedFeastQuery(mDate);
        const fixedSnapshot = await getDocs(fixedFeastQuery);
        diagnostics.fixedFeastResult = fixedSnapshot.docs.map(doc => ({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));

        const dynamicFeastsQueries = getDynamicFeastsQueries(mDate, year);
        diagnostics.dynamicFeastsResults = [];
        if (dynamicFeastsQueries.length > 0) {
            const dynamicSnapshots = await Promise.all(dynamicFeastsQueries.map(q => getDocs(q)));
            dynamicSnapshots.forEach(snapshot => {
                snapshot.forEach(doc => {
                    diagnostics.dynamicFeastsResults.push({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() });
                });
            });
        }

        return {
            diagnostics,
            rawOptions: {
                yearOptions: calendarStore.optionsForYear(year).value,
                dynamicDates: calendarStore.dynamicDatesForYear(year).value
            }
        };
    }

    async function auditYear(year, progressCallback) {
        logger.log(`[Audit] Пачатак праверкі ${year} года`);
        
        await calendarStore.getYearOptions(year);
        await calendarStore.getDynamicDates(year);
        
        const missing = {
            periodDays: [],
            feasts: [],
        };

        const dynamicDates = calendarStore.dynamicDatesForYear(year).value;
        for (const feastKey in feastNameMap) {
            const feastCode = feastNameMap[feastKey];
            const q = query(daysCollection, where('_feast', '==', feastCode));
            const snapshot = await retryFirestore(() => getDocs(q));
            
            if (snapshot.empty) {
                const date = dynamicDates[feastKey] ? dynamicDates[feastKey].format('DD.MM.YYYY') : 'Дата не вылічаная';
                missing.feasts.push({ name: feastCode, date });
            }
        }

        const startDate = moment().year(year).startOf('year');
        const endDate = moment().year(year).endOf('year');
        const totalDays = endDate.diff(startDate, 'days');

        for (let i = 0; i <= totalDays; i++) {
            const currentDate = startDate.clone().add(i, 'days');
            const periodQuery = getPeriodQuery(currentDate, year);

            if (periodQuery) {
                const snapshot = await retryFirestore(() => getDocs(periodQuery));
                if (snapshot.empty) {
                    const params = {
                        date: currentDate.format('DD.MM.YYYY (dddd)'),
                        type: periodQuery._query.filters.find(f => f.field.segments.includes('_periodType'))?.value,
                        week: periodQuery._query.filters.find(f => f.field.segments.includes('_periodWeekNumber'))?.value,
                        day: periodQuery._query.filters.find(f => f.field.segments.includes('_dayOfWeek'))?.value,
                    };
                    logger.warn(`[Audit] НЕ ЗНАЙДЗЕНЫ ДЗЕНЬ ПЕРЫЯДУ:`, params);
                    missing.periodDays.push(params);
                }
            }
            
            if (progressCallback) {
                progressCallback(Math.round((i / totalDays) * 100));
            }
        }
        
        logger.log('[Audit] Праверка завершаная.');
        return missing;
    }

    async function findDayByPeriod(periodType, periodWeekNumber, dayOfWeek) {
        const q = getItemsForPeriodQuery(periodType, dayOfWeek, periodWeekNumber);
        const snapshot = await retryFirestore(() => getDocs(q));
        
        if (snapshot.empty) {
            return null;
        }
        
        const doc = snapshot.docs[0];
        return { id: doc.id, ...doc.data() };
    }

    async function calculateDateForPeriod(year, periodType, periodWeekNumber, dayOfWeek) {
        await calendarStore.getYearOptions(year);
        await calendarStore.getYearOptions(year + 1);
        
        const opts = calendarStore.optionsForYear(year).value;
        const nextYearOpts = calendarStore.optionsForYear(year + 1).value;
        if (!opts) return null;

        const fa = getMoment(opts.firstAdventSunday);
        const christmas = moment(`${year}-12-25`, "YYYY-MM-DD");
        const easter = getMoment(opts.easterSunday);
        const ash = getMoment(opts.ashWednesday);
        const pentecost = getMoment(opts.pentecost);

        let baseDate = null;
        let targetDate = null;

        if (periodType === 'advent') {
            baseDate = fa.clone();
        } else if (periodType === 'christmas') {
            baseDate = christmas.clone();
        } else if (periodType === 'lent') {
            baseDate = ash.clone().add(4, "days");
        } else if (periodType === 'holyweek') {
            baseDate = easter.clone().subtract(1, "week");
        } else if (periodType === 'easter') {
            baseDate = easter.clone();
        } else if (periodType === 'ordinary') {
            const weeksBeforeOrd = parseInt(opts.ordintaryWeeks1) - 1;
            const fo = ash.clone().subtract(2, "days").subtract(weeksBeforeOrd, "weeks");
            const startWeek2 = parseInt(opts.ordintaryWeeks2);
            
            if (periodWeekNumber < parseInt(opts.ordintaryWeeks1)) {
                baseDate = fo.clone();
            } else {
                baseDate = pentecost.clone();
                periodWeekNumber = periodWeekNumber - startWeek2 + 1;
            }
        }

        if (!baseDate) return null;

        const baseDayOfWeek = baseDate.isoWeekday();
        const daysToAdd = (periodWeekNumber - 1) * 7 + (dayOfWeek - baseDayOfWeek);
        targetDate = baseDate.clone().add(daysToAdd, 'days');

        return targetDate.format('YYYY-MM-DD');
    }

    async function getAllFeasts(year) {
        const cacheKey = `year_${year}`;
        if (feastsCache[cacheKey]) {
            logger.log(`[getAllFeasts] Выкарыстоўваем кэш для года ${year}`);
            return feastsCache[cacheKey];
        }

        await calendarStore.getYearOptions(year);
        await calendarStore.getYearOptions(year + 1);
        await calendarStore.getDynamicDates(year);
        
        const dynamicDates = calendarStore.dynamicDatesForYear(year).value || {};
        const allFeasts = [];
        const seenIds = new Set();
        
        const snapshot = await retryFirestore(() => getDocs(daysCollection));
        
        snapshot.forEach(doc => {
            const data = doc.data();
            if ((data._feast || data._feastType) && !seenIds.has(doc.id)) {
                seenIds.add(doc.id);
                allFeasts.push({ id: doc.id, ...data });
            }
        });
        
        const result = allFeasts.map(feast => {
            const isDynamic = Object.keys(feastNameMap).some(key => {
                return feastNameMap[key] === feast._feast && dynamicDates[key];
            });
            
            return {
                ...feast,
                isDynamic,
                isFixed: !isDynamic && feast._date
            };
        });

        feastsCache[cacheKey] = result;
        logger.log(`[getAllFeasts] Загружана і закэшавана ${result.length} праздников для года ${year}`);
        
        return result;
    }

    return { 
        getLiturgicalOptionsForDate, 
        computeLiturgicalOptionsForDate,
        runLiturgicalDiagnostics, 
        auditYear,
        findDayByPeriod,
        calculateDateForPeriod,
        getAllFeasts,
        feastNameMap
    };
}
```

### src/composables/useNotifier.js
```
import { ref } from 'vue';

const snackbar = ref({
  show: false,
  text: '',
  color: 'info',
  timeout: 4000,
});

export function useNotifier() {
  const notify = (text, color = 'info', timeout = 4000) => {
    snackbar.value = {
      show: true,
      text,
      color,
      timeout,
    };
  };

  const success = (text, timeout = 4000) => {
    notify(text, 'success', timeout);
  };

  const error = (text, timeout = 6000) => {
    notify(text, 'error', timeout);
  };

  const warning = (text, timeout = 5000) => {
    notify(text, 'warning', timeout);
  };

  const info = (text, timeout = 4000) => {
    notify(text, 'info', timeout);
  };

  return {
    snackbar,
    notify,
    success,
    error,
    warning,
    info,
  };
}


```

### src/composables/usePrayerPreview.js
```
const previewCache = new Map();

export function getPrayerPreview(text) {
  if (!text) return 'Няма змесціва';
  
  if (previewCache.has(text)) {
    return previewCache.get(text);
  }
  
  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(text, 'text/html');
  const plainText = (doc.body.textContent || "").trim();
  const preview = plainText.length > 150 ? plainText.substring(0, 150) + '...' : plainText;
  
  if (previewCache.size > 100) {
    const firstKey = previewCache.keys().next().value;
    previewCache.delete(firstKey);
  }
  previewCache.set(text, preview);
  
  return preview;
}

export function clearPreviewCache() {
  previewCache.clear();
}


```

export function usePrayers() {

### src/composables/useReadings.js
```
import { computed } from 'vue';
import { storeToRefs } from 'pinia';
import { useReadingsStore } from '@/stores/readings';
import moment from 'moment';

/**
 * Composable для работы с чтениями (readings)
 * Предоставляет удобный интерфейс к readingsStore
 */
export function useReadings() {
  const readingsStore = useReadingsStore();
  
  // Используем storeToRefs для сохранения реактивности
  const { readings, currentReading, isLoading, error } = storeToRefs(readingsStore);

  /**
   * Получить текст текущего чтения
   */
  const readingText = computed(() => {
    if (!currentReading.value) return '';
    
    // Поддержка разных полей для текста (text, content)
    return currentReading.value.text || currentReading.value.content || '';
  });

  /**
   * Получить цикл текущего чтения
   */
  const readingCycle = computed(() => {
    return currentReading.value?.cycle || null;
  });

  /**
   * Проверить, есть ли чтение для текущей даты
   */
  const hasReading = computed(() => {
    return !!currentReading.value;
  });

  /**
   * Загрузить чтения для даты
   */
  async function loadReadingsForDate(date, options = {}) {
    return await readingsStore.getReadingsForDate(date, options);
  }

  /**
   * Получить чтения для сегодняшнего дня
   */
  async function loadReadingsForToday(options = {}) {
    return await loadReadingsForDate(moment().toDate(), options);
  }

  return {
    readings,
    currentReading,
    readingText,
    readingCycle,
    hasReading,
    isLoading,
    error,
    
    loadReadingsForDate,
    loadReadingsForToday,
    
    addReading: readingsStore.addReading,
    updateReading: readingsStore.updateReading,
    deleteReading: readingsStore.deleteReading,
    clearCurrentReading: readingsStore.clearCurrentReading,
  };
}


```

### src/composables/useWakeLock.js
```
import { onUnmounted } from 'vue';
import { logger } from '@/utils/logger';

let wakeLock = null;

const requestWakeLock = async () => {
  if ('wakeLock' in navigator) {
    try {
      wakeLock = await navigator.wakeLock.request('screen');
      logger.log('Screen Wake Lock is active.');
      wakeLock.addEventListener('release', () => {
        logger.log('Screen Wake Lock was released by the system.');
        wakeLock = null;
      });
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error(`Wake Lock request failed: ${err.name}, ${err.message}`);
      throw err;
    }
  } else {
    logger.warn('Wake Lock API is not supported by this browser.');
    throw new Error('Wake Lock API is not supported');
  }
};

const releaseWakeLock = async () => {
  if (wakeLock !== null) {
    try {
      await wakeLock.release();
      wakeLock = null;
      logger.log('Screen Wake Lock released.');
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error(`Wake Lock release failed: ${err.name}, ${err.message}`);
    }
  }
};

export function useWakeLock() {
  onUnmounted(() => {
    releaseWakeLock();
  });

  return {
    requestWakeLock,
    releaseWakeLock,
  };
}

// Экспортируем функции напрямую для использования в stores
export { requestWakeLock, releaseWakeLock };

### src/layouts/default/BottomNav.vue
```
<template>
  <div>
    <v-bottom-navigation 
      :model-value="activeIndex" 
      color="primary" 
      bg-color="surface"
      elevation="4"
      height="64"
    >
      <v-btn 
        v-for="(m, i) in menu" 
        :key="i" 
        :value="i"
        :active="activeIndex === i"
        @click="navigateTo(m.to)"
        variant="text"
        size="large"
        class="bottom-nav-btn"
      >
        <v-icon size="24">{{ m.icon }}</v-icon>
        <span class="text-caption font-weight-medium">{{ m.title }}</span>
      </v-btn>
      
      <v-btn 
        :value="2" 
        :active="activeIndex === 2"
        @click="toggleCalendarDialog()"
        variant="text"
        size="large"
        class="bottom-nav-btn"
      >
        <v-icon size="24">mdi-calendar-month</v-icon>
        <span class="text-caption font-weight-medium">Дзень</span>
      </v-btn>
    </v-bottom-navigation>

    <v-dialog 
      v-model="isCalendarDialogOpen" 
      width="auto" 
      max-width="400"
      :scrim="false"
      location="top"
      :persistent="false"
      :z-index="99"
    >
      <v-card rounded="lg">
        <v-card-title class="d-flex justify-space-between align-center pa-4">
          <span class="text-h6">Выбраць дату</span>
          <v-btn icon="mdi-close" variant="text" size="small" @click="toggleCalendarDialog"></v-btn>
        </v-card-title>
        <v-divider></v-divider>
        <v-card-text class="pa-0">
          <v-date-picker 
            v-model="selectedDate" 
            color="primary"
            hide-header
            :first-day-of-week="1"
          ></v-date-picker>
        </v-card-text>
        <v-divider></v-divider>
        <v-card-actions class="pa-3">
          <v-spacer></v-spacer>
          <v-btn variant="text" @click="toggleCalendarDialog">Адмена</v-btn>
          <v-btn color="primary" variant="flat" @click="loadDay">Выбраць</v-btn>
        </v-card-actions>
      </v-card>
    </v-dialog>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import moment from 'moment'
import 'moment/dist/locale/be'
import { ref, computed, watch } from 'vue'
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { useAppBar } from '@/composables/useAppBar'

const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()
const { isCalendarDialogOpen, toggleCalendarDialog } = useAppBar()
const selectedDate = ref(new Date())

const currentRouteDate = computed(() => {
  if (route.name === 'Today' || route.path === '/') {
    return moment().startOf('day')
  }
  if (route.params.day) {
    return moment(route.params.day, 'YYYY-MM-DD').startOf('day')
  }
  return moment().startOf('day')
})

const today = computed(() => moment().startOf('day'))

const menu = computed(() => [
  {
    title: "Выбраць",
    to: "/picker",
    icon: "mdi-calendar-search"
  },
  {
    title: "Сёння",
    to: "/",
    icon: "mdi-calendar-today"
  },
])

const activeIndex = computed(() => {
  if (route.name === 'LiturgicalPicker') {
    return 0
  }
  
  const routeDate = currentRouteDate.value
  const todayDate = today.value
  
  if (routeDate.isSame(todayDate, 'day')) {
    return 1
  } else {
    return 2
  }
})

function navigateTo(path) {
  router.push(path)
}

watch(isCalendarDialogOpen, (isOpen) => {
  if (isOpen) {
    selectedDate.value = currentRouteDate.value.toDate()
  }
})

function loadDay() {
  toggleCalendarDialog()
  const dateString = moment(selectedDate.value).format('YYYY-MM-DD')
  if (moment(selectedDate.value).isSame(today.value, 'day')) {
    router.push('/')
  } else {
    router.push(`/day/${dateString}`)
  }
}
</script>

<style scoped>
:deep(.v-bottom-navigation) {
  position: fixed !important;
  bottom: 0 !important;
  left: 0 !important;
  right: 0 !important;
  z-index: 100 !important;
}

.bottom-nav-btn {
  min-width: 80px;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 4px;
}

.bottom-nav-btn :deep(.v-btn__content) {
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 4px;
}
</style>

      <v-icon>mdi-format-font</v-icon>
    </v-btn>

     <v-btn v-if="auth && auth.isAdmin" icon @click="goToEdit">
      <v-icon>mdi-pencil-outline</v-icon>
    </v-btn>
    
  </v-app-bar>
</template>

<script setup>
import { useRouter, useRoute } from 'vue-router';
import { useAppBar } from '@/composables/useAppBar';
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth.js';

const router = useRouter();
const route = useRoute();
const { toggleSettingsSheet } = useAppBar();

const auth = useAuthStore();

const goBack = () => {
  if (window.history.length > 1) {
    router.go(-1);
  } else {
    router.push('/');
  }
};

const goToEdit = () => {
  const dayId = route.params.id;
  if (dayId) {
     router.push({ name: 'AdminDayEditor', params: { id: dayId } });
  }
};
</script>

### src/layouts/default/ReadingsBottomNav.vue
```
<template>
  <div>
    <v-bottom-navigation 
      :model-value="activeIndex" 
      color="primary" 
      bg-color="surface"
      elevation="4"
      height="64"
    >
      <v-btn 
        v-for="(m, i) in menu" 
        :key="i" 
        :value="i"
        :active="activeIndex === i"
        @click="navigateTo(m.to)"
        variant="text"
        size="large"
        class="bottom-nav-btn"
      >
        <v-icon size="24">{{ m.icon }}</v-icon>
        <span class="text-caption font-weight-medium">{{ m.title }}</span>
      </v-btn>
      
      <v-btn 
        :value="2" 
        :active="activeIndex === 2"
        @click="toggleCalendarDialog()"
        variant="text"
        size="large"
        class="bottom-nav-btn"
      >
        <v-icon size="24">mdi-calendar-month</v-icon>
        <span class="text-caption font-weight-medium">Дзень</span>
      </v-btn>
    </v-bottom-navigation>

    <v-dialog 
      v-model="isCalendarDialogOpen" 
      width="auto" 
      max-width="400"
      :scrim="false"
      location="top"
      :persistent="false"
      :z-index="99"
    >
      <v-card rounded="lg">
        <v-card-title class="d-flex justify-space-between align-center pa-4">
          <span class="text-h6">Выбраць дату</span>
          <v-btn icon="mdi-close" variant="text" size="small" @click="toggleCalendarDialog"></v-btn>
        </v-card-title>
        <v-divider></v-divider>
        <v-card-text class="pa-0">
          <v-date-picker 
            v-model="selectedDate" 
            color="primary"
            hide-header
            :first-day-of-week="1"
          ></v-date-picker>
        </v-card-text>
        <v-divider></v-divider>
        <v-card-actions class="pa-3">
          <v-spacer></v-spacer>
          <v-btn variant="text" @click="toggleCalendarDialog">Адмена</v-btn>
          <v-btn color="primary" variant="flat" @click="loadDay">Выбраць</v-btn>
        </v-card-actions>
      </v-card>
    </v-dialog>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import moment from 'moment'
import 'moment/dist/locale/be'
import { ref, computed, watch } from 'vue'
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { useAppBar } from '@/composables/useAppBar'

const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()
const { isCalendarDialogOpen, toggleCalendarDialog } = useAppBar()
const selectedDate = ref(new Date())

const currentRouteDate = computed(() => {
  if (route.name === 'Readings') {
    return moment().startOf('day')
  }
  if (route.params.date) {
    return moment(route.params.date, 'YYYY-MM-DD').startOf('day')
  }
  return moment().startOf('day')
})

const today = computed(() => moment().startOf('day'))

const menu = computed(() => [
  {
    title: "Выбраць",
    to: "/readings/picker",
    icon: "mdi-calendar-search"
  },
  {
    title: "Сёння",
    to: "/readings",
    icon: "mdi-calendar-today"
  },
])

const activeIndex = computed(() => {
  if (route.name === 'ReadingsPicker') {
    return 0
  }
  
  const routeDate = currentRouteDate.value
  const todayDate = today.value
  
  if (routeDate.isSame(todayDate, 'day')) {
    return 1
  } else {
    return 2
  }
})

function navigateTo(path) {
  router.push(path)
}

watch(isCalendarDialogOpen, (isOpen) => {
  if (isOpen) {
    selectedDate.value = currentRouteDate.value.toDate()
  }
})

function loadDay() {
  toggleCalendarDialog()
  const dateString = moment(selectedDate.value).format('YYYY-MM-DD')
  if (moment(selectedDate.value).isSame(today.value, 'day')) {
    router.push('/readings')
  } else {
    router.push(`/readings/${dateString}`)
  }
}
</script>

<style scoped>
:deep(.v-bottom-navigation) {
  position: fixed !important;
  bottom: 0 !important;
  left: 0 !important;
  right: 0 !important;
  z-index: 100 !important;
}

.bottom-nav-btn {
  min-width: 80px;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 4px;
}

.bottom-nav-btn :deep(.v-btn__content) {
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 4px;
}
</style>



```

  webFontLoader.load({
    google: {
      families: ['Roboto:100,300,400,500,700,900&display=swap'],
    },
  })

    ],
  },
];

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(process.env.BASE_URL),
  routes,
  scrollBehavior(to, from, savedPosition) {
    if (savedPosition) {
      return savedPosition;
    }
    return { top: 0, left: 0, behavior: 'smooth' };
  },
});

router.beforeEach((to, from, next) => {
  const authStore = useAuthStore();
  const requiresAuth = to.matched.some(record => record.meta.requiresAuth);
  if (requiresAuth && !authStore.isAdmin) {
    next({ name: 'Login' });
  } else {
    next();
  }
});

export default router;

### src/services/prayerService.js
```
import { db } from '@/plugins/firebase';
import { 
  collection, 
  addDoc, 
  deleteDoc, 
  updateDoc, 
  doc,
  serverTimestamp,
  query,
  orderBy
} from 'firebase/firestore';
import { logger } from '@/utils/logger';
import { retryFirestore } from '@/utils/retry';

const PRAYERS_COLLECTION = 'prayers';

export function getPrayersCollection() {
  return collection(db, PRAYERS_COLLECTION);
}

export function getPrayersQuery() {
  try {
    return query(getPrayersCollection(), orderBy('createdAt', 'desc'));
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('[prayerService] Памылка стварэння запыту, спрабую без orderBy:', error);
    // Fallback: если нет индекса, возвращаем запрос без сортировки
    return query(getPrayersCollection());
  }
}

export async function addPrayer(data) {
  try {
    const docRef = await retryFirestore(() => addDoc(getPrayersCollection(), {
      ...data,
      createdAt: serverTimestamp()
    }));
    logger.log('[prayerService] Малітва дададзена:', docRef.id);
    return docRef;
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('[prayerService] Памылка дадавання малітвы:', error);
    throw error;
  }
}

export async function deletePrayer(id) {
  try {
    await retryFirestore(() => deleteDoc(doc(db, PRAYERS_COLLECTION, id)));
    logger.log('[prayerService] Малітва выдалена:', id);
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('[prayerService] Памылка выдалення малітвы:', error);
    throw error;
  }
}

export async function updatePrayer(id, data) {
  try {
    const dataToUpdate = { ...data };
    delete dataToUpdate.id;
    await retryFirestore(() => updateDoc(doc(db, PRAYERS_COLLECTION, id), dataToUpdate));
    logger.log('[prayerService] Малітва абноўлена:', id);
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('[prayerService] Памылка абнаўлення малітвы:', error);
    throw error;
  }
}


```

### src/services/readingsService.js
```
import { db } from '@/plugins/firebase';
import { 
  collection, 
  query, 
  where, 
  getDocs,
  getDoc,
  addDoc,
  updateDoc,
  deleteDoc,
  doc,
  serverTimestamp,
  orderBy,
} from 'firebase/firestore';
import { logger } from '@/utils/logger';
import { retryFirestore } from '@/utils/retry';
import { validateReading, normalizeReading } from '@/utils/readingsValidator';
import { getCachedReadings, cacheReadings } from '@/utils/readingsCache';
import moment from 'moment';

const READINGS_COLLECTION = 'readings';
const READINGS_DATA_COLLECTION = 'readingsData'; // Старая коллекция с существующими данными

/**
 * Получить коллекцию readings
 */
export function getReadingsCollection() {
  return collection(db, READINGS_COLLECTION);
}

/**
 * Получить коллекцию readingsData (старая коллекция)
 */
export function getReadingsDataCollection() {
  return collection(db, READINGS_DATA_COLLECTION);
}

/**
 * Поиск чтений по периоду и дню недели
 */
export function getReadingsByPeriodQuery(periodType, periodWeekNumber, dayOfWeek, cycle) {
  const conditions = [
    where('_periodType', '==', periodType),
    where('_periodWeekNumber', '==', periodWeekNumber),
    where('_dayOfWeek', '==', dayOfWeek),
  ];
  
  if (cycle) {
    conditions.push(where('cycle', '==', cycle));
  }
  
  return query(getReadingsCollection(), ...conditions);
}

/**
 * Поиск чтений по дате
 */
export function getReadingsByDateQuery(date, cycle) {
  const dateStr = moment(date).format('YYYY-MM-DD');
  const conditions = [where('_date', '==', dateStr)];
  
  if (cycle) {
    conditions.push(where('cycle', '==', cycle));
  }
  
  return query(getReadingsCollection(), ...conditions);
}

/**
 * Поиск чтений по празднику
 */
export function getReadingsByFeastQuery(feastId, feastDate, cycle) {
  const dateStr = moment(feastDate).format('YYYY-MM-DD');
  const conditions = [
    where('feastId', '==', feastId),
    where('feastDate', '==', dateStr),
  ];
  
  if (cycle) {
    conditions.push(where('cycle', '==', cycle));
  }
  
  return query(getReadingsCollection(), ...conditions);
}

/**
 * Получить все чтения из коллекции readingsData
 * @returns {Promise<Array>} Массив всех чтений из readingsData
 */
export async function getAllReadingsData() {
  try {
    const snapshot = await retryFirestore(() => getDocs(getReadingsDataCollection()));
    const results = [];
    snapshot.forEach(doc => {
      const data = doc.data();
      results.push({
        id: doc.id,
        text: data.text || data.content || '', // Поддержка разных полей
        _source: 'readingsData', // Метка источника
        ...data
      });
    });
    return results;
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('[readingsService] Памылка атрымання readingsData:', error);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Поиск в readingsData по дате (поле day в формате DD.MM.YYYY)
 * @param {string} dateStr - Дата в формате YYYY-MM-DD
 * @returns {Promise<Array>} Массив найденных чтений
 */
export async function searchReadingsDataByDate(dateStr) {
  try {
    // Преобразуем YYYY-MM-DD в разные форматы для поиска в readingsData
    const mDate = moment(dateStr, 'YYYY-MM-DD');
    if (!mDate.isValid()) {
      logger.error('[readingsService] Няверны фармат даты для пошуку ў readingsData:', dateStr);
      return [];
    }
    
    // Пробуем оба формата: с ведущими нулями и без
    const dayFormatWithZeros = mDate.format('DD.MM.YYYY'); // 23.04.2022
    const dayFormatWithoutZeros = mDate.format('D.M.YYYY'); // 23.4.2022
    
    logger.log('[readingsService] Пошук ў readingsData па даце:', dayFormatWithZeros, 'або', dayFormatWithoutZeros, '(з', dateStr, ')');
    
    // Пробуем найти по обоим форматам
    const queries = [
      query(getReadingsDataCollection(), where('day', '==', dayFormatWithZeros)),
      query(getReadingsDataCollection(), where('day', '==', dayFormatWithoutZeros))
    ];
    
    const snapshots = await Promise.all(
      queries.map(q => retryFirestore(() => getDocs(q)))
    );
    
    const results = [];
    const seenIds = new Set();
    
    snapshots.forEach((snapshot, index) => {
      snapshot.forEach(doc => {
        if (!seenIds.has(doc.id)) {
          seenIds.add(doc.id);
          const data = doc.data();
          results.push({
            id: doc.id,
            text: data.text || data.content || '',
            _source: 'readingsData',
            day: data.day, // Сохраняем оригинальную дату
            ...data
          });
        }
      });
    });
    
    logger.log('[readingsService] Знойдзена ў readingsData:', results.length, 'чытанняў для даты', dayFormatWithZeros);
    return results;
  } catch (error) {
    // Если поле day не существует или запрос не удался
    logger.error('[readingsService] Памылка пошуку ў readingsData:', error.message, error);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Комплексный поиск чтений для конкретной даты
 * Сначала ищет в новой коллекции readings, затем в старой readingsData
 * @param {Date|moment.Moment|string} date - Дата
 * @param {Object} options - Опции поиска
 * @param {string} options.cycle - Цикл (A/B/C/1/2)
 * @param {string} options.periodType - Тип периода
 * @param {number} options.periodWeekNumber - Номер недели в периоде
 * @param {number} options.dayOfWeek - День недели (1-7)
 * @param {string} options.feastId - ID праздника
 * @param {boolean} options.useCache - Использовать кэш (по умолчанию true)
 * @param {boolean} options.includeReadingsData - Искать также в readingsData (по умолчанию true)
 * @returns {Promise<Array>} Массив найденных чтений
 */
export async function getReadingsForDate(date, options = {}) {
  const { cycle, periodType, periodWeekNumber, dayOfWeek, feastId, useCache = true, includeReadingsData = true } = options;
  const mDate = moment(date).startOf('day');
  const dateStr = mDate.format('YYYY-MM-DD');
  
  // Проверяем кэш
  if (useCache) {
    const cached = getCachedReadings(date, options);
    if (cached !== null) {
      logger.log('[readingsService] Выкарыстаны кэш для даты:', dateStr);
      return cached;
    }
  }
  
  const queries = [];
  
  // 1. Поиск в новой коллекции readings
  // 1.1. По дате
  queries.push(getReadingsByDateQuery(dateStr, cycle));
  
  // 1.2. По периоду (если указаны параметры)
  if (periodType && periodWeekNumber !== undefined && dayOfWeek) {
    queries.push(getReadingsByPeriodQuery(periodType, periodWeekNumber, dayOfWeek, cycle));
  }
  
  // 1.3. По празднику (если указан)
  if (feastId) {
    queries.push(getReadingsByFeastQuery(feastId, dateStr, cycle));
  }
  
  try {
    const querySnapshots = await Promise.all(
      queries.map(q => retryFirestore(() => getDocs(q)))
    );
    
    const results = [];
    querySnapshots.forEach(snapshot => {
      snapshot.forEach(doc => {
        results.push({
          id: doc.id,
          _source: 'readings', // Метка источника
          ...doc.data()
        });
      });
    });
    
    // 2. Поиск в старой коллекции readingsData (если включено)
    if (includeReadingsData) {
      try {
        const readingsDataResults = await searchReadingsDataByDate(dateStr);
        logger.log('[readingsService] Вынікі з readingsData:', readingsDataResults.length);
        if (readingsDataResults.length > 0) {
          results.push(...readingsDataResults);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        logger.warn('[readingsService] Памылка пошуку ў readingsData:', error);
      }
    }
    
    logger.log('[readingsService] Усяго знойдзена да аб\'яднання:', results.length);
    
    // Удаляем дубликаты по ID
    const uniqueResults = Array.from(
      new Map(results.map(item => [item.id, item])).values()
    );
    
    logger.log('[readingsService] Пасля аб\'яднання:', uniqueResults.length, 'чытанняў');
    
    // Приоритет: readings (новая коллекция) > readingsData (старая)
    // НО если есть readingsData и нет readings, используем readingsData
    // Сортируем так, чтобы readings были первыми, но readingsData тоже учитывались
    uniqueResults.sort((a, b) => {
      if (a._source === 'readings' && b._source === 'readingsData') return -1;
      if (a._source === 'readingsData' && b._source === 'readings') return 1;
      return 0;
    });
    
    // Сохраняем в кэш
    if (useCache) {
      cacheReadings(date, options, uniqueResults);
    }
    
    logger.log('[readingsService] Фінальны вынік для даты', dateStr, ':', uniqueResults.length, 'чытанняў');
    
    return uniqueResults;
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('[readingsService] Памылка пошуку чытанняў:', error);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Добавить новое чтение
 * @param {Object} data - Данные чтения
 * @returns {Promise<DocumentReference>} Ссылка на созданный документ
 * @throws {Error} Если валидация не прошла или произошла ошибка сохранения
 */
export async function addReading(data) {
  // Валидация данных
  const validation = validateReading(data);
  if (validation !== true) {
    const error = new Error(`Валідацыя не прайшла: ${validation}`);
    logger.error('[readingsService]', error);
    throw error;
  }

  try {
    // Нормализация данных
    const normalizedData = normalizeReading(data);
    
    const docRef = await retryFirestore(() => addDoc(getReadingsCollection(), {
      ...normalizedData,
      createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    }));
    logger.log('[readingsService] Чытанне дададзена:', docRef.id);
    return docRef;
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('[readingsService] Памылка дадавання чытання:', error);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Обновить чтение
 * @param {string} id - ID документа
 * @param {Object} data - Данные для обновления
 * @throws {Error} Если валидация не прошла или произошла ошибка обновления
 */
export async function updateReading(id, data) {
  // Валидация данных (частичная валидация для обновлений)
  const validation = validateReading(data, { partial: true });
  if (validation !== true) {
    const error = new Error(`Валідацыя не прайшла: ${validation}`);
    logger.error('[readingsService]', error);
    throw error;
  }

  try {
    const dataToUpdate = { ...data };
    delete dataToUpdate.id;
    
    // Нормализация данных
    const normalizedData = normalizeReading(dataToUpdate);
    normalizedData.updatedAt = serverTimestamp();
    
    await retryFirestore(() => updateDoc(doc(db, READINGS_COLLECTION, id), normalizedData));
    logger.log('[readingsService] Чытанне абноўлена:', id);
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('[readingsService] Памылка абнаўлення чытання:', error);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Удалить чтение
 */
export async function deleteReading(id) {
  try {
    await retryFirestore(() => deleteDoc(doc(db, READINGS_COLLECTION, id)));
    logger.log('[readingsService] Чытанне выдалена:', id);
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('[readingsService] Памылка выдалення чытання:', error);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Получить чтение по ID
 */
export async function getReadingById(id) {
  try {
    const docRef = doc(db, READINGS_COLLECTION, id);
    const docSnap = await retryFirestore(() => getDoc(docRef));
    
    if (docSnap.exists()) {
      return {
        id: docSnap.id,
        ...docSnap.data()
      };
    }
    
    return null;
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('[readingsService] Памылка атрымання чытання:', error);
    throw error;
  }
}

### src/stores/auth.js
```
import { defineStore } from 'pinia';
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { getAuth, onAuthStateChanged, signInWithEmailAndPassword, signOut } from 'firebase/auth';
import router from '@/router';
import { logger } from '@/utils/logger';
import { retryFirestore } from '@/utils/retry';
import { ADMIN_UIDS } from '@/constants/admin';

export const useAuthStore = defineStore('auth', () => {
  const user = ref(null);
  const loading = ref(true);

  const isAdmin = computed(() => user.value && ADMIN_UIDS.includes(user.value.uid));

  const init = () => {
    const auth = getAuth();
    onAuthStateChanged(auth, (firebaseUser) => {
      user.value = firebaseUser;
      loading.value = false;
    });
  };

  const login = async (email, password) => {
    const auth = getAuth();
    try {
      await retryFirestore(
        () => signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password)
      );
      setTimeout(() => {
        router.push('/');
      }, 100);
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error("Памылка ўваходу:", error);
      throw error;
    }
  };

  const logout = async () => {
    const auth = getAuth();
    await signOut(auth);
    router.push('/');
  };

  return { user, loading, isAdmin, init, login, logout };
});
```

### src/stores/calendar.js
```
import { defineStore } from 'pinia';
import { ref, computed  } from 'vue';
import { collection, query, where, getDocs, getFirestore } from 'firebase/firestore';
import moment from 'moment';
import 'moment/dist/locale/be';
import { logger } from '@/utils/logger';
import { retryFirestore } from '@/utils/retry';

export const useCalendarStore = defineStore('calendar', () => {
    const db = getFirestore();
    moment.locale('be');

    const yearOptionsCache = ref({});
    const dynamicDatesCache = ref({});

    async function getYearOptions(year) {
        if (yearOptionsCache.value[year]) {
            return yearOptionsCache.value[year];
        }
        
        logger.log(`[CalendarStore] Пошук наладаў для ${year} года ў Firestore...`);
        const q = query(collection(db, 'yearOptions'), where('year', '==', Number(year)));
        
        const querySnapshot = await retryFirestore(() => getDocs(q));
        
        if (querySnapshot.empty) {
            logger.error(`[CalendarStore] Налады для года ${year} не знойдзены!`);
            return null;
        }
        
        const options = querySnapshot.docs[0].data();
        yearOptionsCache.value[year] = options;
        logger.log(`[CalendarStore] Налады для года ${year} паспяхова загружаны.`);
        return options;
    }

    async function getDynamicDates(year) {
    logger.log(`[CalendarStore] Вычисление динамических дат для ${year} года...`);
    
    await getYearOptions(year); 
    const opts = yearOptionsCache.value[year];
    if (!opts) {
        logger.error(`[CalendarStore] Не удалось получить опции для ${year} года. Вычисление дат прервано.`);
        return {};
    }
    const dates = {};
    const getMoment = (isoDateStr) => moment(isoDateStr, 'YYYY-MM-DD');
    const easter = getMoment(opts.easterSunday);
    const pentecost = getMoment(opts.pentecost);
    const firstAdventSunday = getMoment(opts.firstAdventSunday);
    // Расчет дат до Пасхи
    dates.ashWednesday = getMoment(opts.ashWednesday);
    dates.palmSunday = easter.clone().subtract(7, 'days'); // ✅ ВЕРБНОЕ ВОСКРЕСЕНЬЕ
    // Расчет дат после Пасхи
    dates.easterSunday = easter;
    dates.pentecost = pentecost;
    dates.ascension = pentecost.clone().subtract(7, 'days'); // ✅ ВОЗНЕСЕНИЕ (7-е воскресенье Пасхи)
    
    const holyTrinitySunday = pentecost.clone().add(7, 'days');
    dates.holyTrinity = holyTrinitySunday;
    dates.corpusChristi = holyTrinitySunday.clone().add(4, 'days'); // ✅ ТЕЛО И КРОВЬ ХРИСТА (четверг после Троицы)
    // Расчет дат в "обычное время"
    dates.holyFamily = getMoment(opts.holyFamily);
    dates.baptismOfLord = getMoment(opts.baptismOfLord);
    dates.christKing = firstAdventSunday.clone().subtract(7, 'days');
    const sacredHeartOfJesus = pentecost.clone().add(19, 'days');
    dates.sacredHeartOfJesus = sacredHeartOfJesus;
    dates.immaculateHeartOfMary = sacredHeartOfJesus.clone().add(1, 'day'); // Следующий день
    dates.christTheHighPriest = pentecost.clone().subtract(11, 'days'); // Пн после Пятидесятницы - уточнить если нужно
    dynamicDatesCache.value[year] = dates;
    logger.log(`[CalendarStore] Динамические даты для ${year} года вычислены.`);
    return dates;
}

    // --- Функция для получения всех доступных годов из Firestore ---
    async function getAvailableYears() {
        try {
            const q = query(collection(db, 'yearOptions'));
            const snapshot = await retryFirestore(() => getDocs(q));
            const years = [];
            snapshot.forEach(doc => {
                const data = doc.data();
                if (data.year) {
                    years.push(data.year);
                }
            });
            return years.sort((a, b) => b - a); // Сортируем по убыванию (новые первыми)
        } catch (error) {
            logger.error('[CalendarStore] Памылка пры атрыманні спісу гадоў:', error);
            return [];
        }
    }

    return { 
        getYearOptions, 
        getDynamicDates,
        getAvailableYears,
        optionsForYear: (year) => computed(() => yearOptionsCache.value[year]),
        dynamicDatesForYear: (year) => computed(() => dynamicDatesCache.value[year])
    };
});
```

### src/stores/day.js
```
// stores/fonts.js
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'

import { useCollection, useFirestore } from 'vuefire'
import { collection, orderBy, query, doc, updateDoc, deleteDoc, setDoc, addDoc } from 'firebase/firestore'

export const useDayStore = defineStore('day', {
    state: () => {
        return {
            dayToEdit: {},
            dayToDelete: {}
        }
    },
    actions: {
        setDayToEdit(dte) {
            this.dayToEdit = dte;
        },
        async updateDay(dte) {
            this.dayToEdit = dte;
            
            const db = useFirestore()
            const docRef = doc(db, "days", `${dte.id}`);

            await updateDoc(docRef, {
                ...dte
            });
        },
        async deleteDay(dte) {
            this.dayToDelete = dte;
            
            const db = useFirestore()
            const docRef = doc(db, "days", `${dte.id}`);

            await deleteDoc(docRef, {
                ...dte
            });
        }
    },

    getters: {
        getDayToEdit: (state) => {
            return state.dayToEdit;
        }
    },
})
